{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"gigs","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"I caught one of the Rolling Stones’ first gigs in Richmond.","type":"example"},{"text":"Hey, when are we gonna get that hotel gig again?","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Originally (music), a performing engagement by a musician or musical group; (by extension, film, television, theater) a job or role for a performer."],"tags":["informal"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I had this gig as a file clerk but it wasn’t my style so I left.","type":"example"},{"text":"That guy’s got a great gig over at the bike shop. {"etymology_number":"2","forms":[{"form":"la","tags":["alternative"]},{"form":"lar","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"particle","senses":[{"glosses":["Placed at the end of a sentence to accentuate the mood or attitude of the speaker."]},{"examples":[{"text":"You won’t drown one lah. The water is very shallow.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Placed at the end of a sentence to convey reassurance or express solidarity."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Just try your best lah, and don’t think too much about it.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Indicates an attempt at expressing sympathy, conveying informality or sincerity, or establishing a rapport with the listener."]},{"examples":[{"text":"— I don’t think you should be cutting corners, at least put in some effort and make it look legit.\n— They won’t care one lah... I don’t have all day...","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Placed at the end of an assertive statement to express denial or dismissiveness."]},{"examples":[{"text":"These shoes are too small lah.","type":"example"},{"text":"No need to count lah. I’m sure the number is right.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used after an assertive statement to reinforce its authoritativeness, or to show that the speaker is confident with what they are saying."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Get a whiteboard marker and write over it lah.","type":"example"},{"text":"They’re not wrong lah... But they’re only taking themselves into consideration.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Asserts that something is clear, obvious or straightforward."],"synonyms":[{"word":"lor"}]},{"glosses":["Used to tone down an imperative sentence, so it sounds more like a request or suggestion."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Don’t waste your time there lah.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Indicates strong persuasion at the end of an imperative sentence."]},{"examples":[{"text":"— Not many people would think of using a pressure cooker.\n— I mean, it’s pretty unconventional lah, I have to admit.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Indicates a retrospective remark or a reassessment of one’s opinion."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The nearest train station is 15 minutes away. I think we take the bus lah, hor?","type":"example"},{"text":"Use the mittens lah.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Reinforces a suggestion with the implication that it is the more practical option."]},{"examples":[{"text":"In other words, you’re the one who started it lah!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Indicates confirmation-seeking at the end of an inferential statement."]},{"examples":[{"text":"So in the end it wasn’t my fault lah!","type":"example"},{"text":"Do it yourself lah! Always need me to help is it?","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to express realisation, or vexation when something is already obvious."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Oh wait it’s not today, lah. It’s on Sunday.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Reinforces the factuality of an assertive statement made to correct an inaccurate, underlying assumption."],"tags":["uncommon"]},{"glosses":["Used to convey a slight sense of dissatisfaction or irritation."],"tags":["uncommon"]},{"glosses":["Used for enumeration (when listing examples)."],"tags":["uncommon"]},{"glosses":["An assertive separator, used to reverse the order of the usual topic–comment structure of a sentence."],"raw_tags":["in Singapore"],"tags":["Malaysia","uncommon"]},{"glosses":["Used sarcastically."],"tags":["uncommon"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/lɑː/","tags":["Malaysia","Singapore"]},{"ipa":"[lä(ː)]","tags":["Malaysia","Singapore"]},{"ipa":"[lɑh˨]"},{"ipa":"/lɑː/"}],"upstream_line":165464,"upstream_offset":771723406,"upstream_record_sha256":"5344b9b20d047d41fc1b6f93b4786c15f87c1775a4da0d41c390a16b6f5d4b87","word":"lah"}
